=== PDFPAGE 1
The
SECONA
power
A mathematical analysis of the letters
attributed to the Zodiac murderer

and supplement to TIMES 17
by Gareth Penn
€ 1999 Gareth Penn
=== PDFPAGE 2
Abstract
The notorious Zodiac murderer (fl. 1966-1974, California)
left a legacy of two dozen letters, most of them addressed
to the San Francisco Chronicle. In these letters, which
covered four ciphers, he claimed to be revealing his iden-
tity in “code.” Whereas cryptanalytical effort has hereto-
fore concentrated on the four ciphers, I analyze the letters
using a digital approach (Morse Code and base-two num-
bers), as well as reformatting them according to the mod-
el provided by the ciphers (17-column rectangle). Several
tutorials disclosed by this analysis confirm that the Zodiac
literature can be read through the application of this
methodology. I then compare patterns discovered in the
Zodiac letters with similar patterns disclosed by the same
analytical means in the published writings of a known in-
dividual. I offer autobiographical statements common to
both authors as motivation for the Zodiac crimes. Finally,
I present a comparison of statements on miscellaneous
themes common to both authors.
